# Skills · D · part 129

760 entries, doubt-driven-development to doxygen-generator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **doubt-driven-development**: 對非平凡決策啟動新鮮 context 對抗審查（找碴不背書），在修正還便宜的時候抓出錯誤方向。適用：高風險改動（production、資安敏感邏輯、不可逆操作）、不熟的程式碼、要宣稱「這樣是安全的 / 可行的」之前。機械性操作與一行修改不適用。 ([seikaikyo/dash-skills](https://github.com/seikaikyo/dash-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development) · ★2)
- **doubt-driven-development**: Cross-examines a non-trivial decision or plan in a fresh context before it is locked in — surfacing hidden failure modes and unstated assumptions. Use before high-stakes or hard-to-reverse decisions (auth, security, migrations, irreversible operations). For reviewing finished code, use code-review-and-quality. ([vargos-labs/vargos](https://github.com/vargos-labs/vargos/tree/HEAD/.templates/agent/skills/doubt-driven-development) · ★2)
- **doubt-driven-development**: Challenge assumptions and hidden dependencies before they become bugs ([vignesh2027/AI-AGENT-SKILLS](https://github.com/vignesh2027/AI-AGENT-SKILLS/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **doubt-driven-development**: Revisión adversarial in-flight de decisiones no triviales: CLAIM → EXTRACT → DOUBT → RECONCILE → STOP. Trigger: doubt-driven, alta stakes, seguridad, irreversible, código unfamiliar, antes de commit crítico. ([Abrahan-Eagle/zonix-eats-front](https://github.com/Abrahan-Eagle/zonix-eats-front/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doubt-driven-development) · ★1)
- **doubt-driven-development**: Подвергает каждое нетривиальное решение состязательному ревью на свежем контексте, прежде чем оно устоится. Используй, когда корректность важнее скорости, когда работаешь в незнакомом коде, когда ставки высоки (продакшн, логика, чувствительная к безопасности, необратимые операции) или каждый раз, когда уверенный вывод дешевле проверить сейчас, чем отлаживать потом. ([Aleksandr-Litvinenko/agent-skills-ru](https://github.com/Aleksandr-Litvinenko/agent-skills-ru/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development) · ★1)
- **doubt-driven-development**: Cross-examine one non-trivial in-flight decision before it stands — name the claim, hand a fresh code-cold agent the artifact and its contract with your reasoning stripped, classify what comes back, stop at three cycles. Not the merge-gate diff pass (code-review) or a failure already in hand (debugging-and-error-recovery). - **doubt-driven-development**: Adversarially reviews non-trivial decisions in fresh context before they stand. Use for material claims involving high stakes, uncertain boundaries, irreversible effects, or unfamiliar behavior that evidence does not yet establish. Do not use for mechanical or explicitly speed-first work. - **doubt-driven-review**: Adversarial fresh-context review for non-trivial decisions before they stand. Use for production-impacting logic, security-sensitive changes, unfamiliar code, or high-blast-radius architecture choices. Triggers: "doubt check", "adversarial review", "challenge this decision", "second look". ([charlieviettq/awesome-agent-skill](https://github.com/charlieviettq/awesome-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/core-workflow/doubt-driven-review) · ★27)
- **doubt-driven-review**: Adversarial fresh-context review for non-trivial decisions before they stand. Use for production-impacting logic, security-sensitive changes, unfamiliar code, or high-blast-radius architecture choices. ([charlieviettq/awesome-agent-skill](https://github.com/charlieviettq/awesome-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/doubt-driven-review) · ★27)
- **doubt-driven-review**: Use when a consequential decision or uncertain correctness claim needs an independent challenge before implementation or handoff. Routine changes do not require a separate reviewer or model. ([caiolombello/skills](https://github.com/caiolombello/skills/tree/HEAD/doubt-driven-review))
- **doubt-driven-review**: Use as a gate on any non-trivial decision that constrains the future - a contract boundary, storage choice, concurrency model, or contentious FR scope. Runs an adversarial CLAIM→EXTRACT→DOUBT→RECONCILE pass; routes the rationale + rejected alternative into the task Notes and doc revision history. ([septimas7/application_design_build](https://github.com/septimas7/application_design_build/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-review))
- **doubt-driven-skill**: Subjects every non-trivial decision to a fresh-context adversarial review before it stands. Use when correctness matters more than speed, when working in unfamiliar code, when stakes are high (production, security-sensitive logic, irreversible operations), or any time a confident output would be cheaper to verify now than to debug later. ([kinqsradiollc/BrainRouter](https://github.com/kinqsradiollc/BrainRouter/tree/HEAD/skills/agent/doubt-driven-skill) · ★3)
- **doubt-driven-zh**: 质疑驱动开发、对非平凡决策做冷上下文的对抗性复审、在提交前找漏洞。当用户说「帮我挑刺」「这段代码/方案哪里会出问题」「上线前多一双眼睛」「我不太确定这个改动安全」「让另一个模型审一下」「高风险迁移先审」，或改动涉及分支逻辑、跨模块边界、编译器验不了的属性（线程安全、幂等、顺序）、不可逆操作时使用。五步：CLAIM 写清主张与为何重要 → EXTRACT 剥出最小可审单元（产物 + 契约，不带你的推理）→ DOUBT 用"找问题、不要认可"的对抗提示交给冷上下文审稿者（只给产物与契约，不给主张；交互式必须提供跨模型二审的选项）→ RECONCILE 逐条按 契约误读/有效可行动/有效权衡/噪音 分类 → STOP 只剩琐碎、3 轮或用户拍板即停。改编自 addyosmani/agent-skills 的 doubt-driven-development（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/doubt-driven-zh) · ★4)
- **doubt-escalation**: In-flight escalation (Doubt model) for the dowiz/DeliveryOS harness. When an OBSERVABLE signal fires mid-task (a loop of N failed attempts, a red-line/irreversible edit, ≥2 surviving interpretations, evidence conflict, novelty, or a result that contradicts a stated expectation), climb the cheapest-sufficient escalation rung within budget K — self-divergence → specialist/research subagent → stronger model → council → human — then RESUME the main task. Friction, not a hard stop, except irreversible/red-line which gate to a human. Routine reversible low-uncertainty work never escalates. ([SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz](https://github.com/SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/doubt-escalation) · ★2)
- **doubt-escalation**: How any agent resolves doubts without guessing: triage (resolve, assume or escalate), a doubt taxonomy with a routing table to quick-researcher, deep-researcher, fact-checker, tester, quality-control, code-quality-analyst, adversarial-verifier, file-summarizer or the caller, the doubt brief template, cost limits, integrating answers and recording unresolved doubts. Preloaded into every agent. ([rustcircle/c101](https://github.com/rustcircle/c101/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/doubt-escalation))
- **doubt-gate**: Cross-examine a non-trivial decision in-flight — before it stands — with a fresh-context reviewer biased to disprove. The in-flight complement to adversarial-verify (which refutes finished output). Use for branching logic, boundary crossings, properties the compiler can't check (thread-safety, idempotence, ordering, invariants), or irreversible blast radius (deploy, migration, public API). ([jpoindexter/Vanta](https://github.com/jpoindexter/Vanta/tree/HEAD/vanta-ts/skills-library/doubt-gate) · ★4)
- **doubt-it**: Adversarially check a claim before acting on it, using a fresh-context subagent that tries to prove it wrong. Use when about to take a risky or hard-to-reverse step, when a diagnosis or "this is safe" conclusion rests on reasoning rather than evidence, or when the user asks to double-check or sanity-check a conclusion. - **doubt-pass**: Adversarial doubt cycle on a plan or design before any code exists — distinct from post-hoc code review. Use when the user asks to doubt this, stress-test a design before building it, poke holes in a plan, find the flaw in an approach, or before committing to a non-trivial architecture or design decision. ([raisedadead/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/raisedadead/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/skills/doubt-pass) · ★3)
- **doubt-pass**: Fresh-context adversarial judge of a decision-ledger candidate. Launched by the /plan orchestrator pre-freeze on irreversible-blast-radius triggers (migrations, security, or rollback surfaces); also invocable when the operator asks to stress a decision set. Reads only the intent, surface map, and bare decisions — never the author's rationale — and reports what is wrong, missing, or unconsidered. - **doubt-review**: Puts a non-trivial in-flight decision through a fresh-context adversarial reviewer before it stands, then reconciles the findings. - **doubting-results**: Checks a model result before it is reported, compared or acted on. Catches scores too good to be true, leaked features, shuffled time splits, a test set used more than once, metrics computed on training data, comparisons on different rows or at different thresholds, accuracy on rare positives, missing intervals, slices too small to judge, labels written by a rule or by the model itself, and rules changed after their result was seen. - **doudou-dev**: 跨平台（Windows、macOS、Linux）管理 doudou-ai-coding 开发与构建流程。包含两大核心模式：(1) 开发调试模式（通过“调试/开发/启动/运行/本地跑起来/dev”等触发），启动 doudou-eggjs (npm run dev) 与 doudou-vue3 (pnpm dev)，提供端口与环境探测、依赖自动安装、并发日志与优雅退出；(2) 生产打包模式（通过“打包/构建/编译产物/生产打包/build/build:prod”等触发），执行 doudou-eggjs (npm run build，即 rec build -o dist) 与 doudou-vue3 (pnpm run build:prod，即 vite build) 生成发布产物。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-dev) · ★209 · 3 copies)
- **doudou-doc**: 把办公文档转成 Markdown（Office / 电子书 / PDF → Markdown）。支持 Word（doc/docx/odt/rtf）、PPT（ppt/pptx/odp）、表格（xlsx/ods/csv）、以及 pdf 和 epub。当用户提供本地文档文件、文档目录或远程文档 URL，并希望提取正文、转成 Markdown、喂给大模型阅读总结、抽取表格数据、或把一批合同/报表/论文批量转文本时使用此技能。注意本技能不做 OCR：扫描件与纯图片 PDF 没有文字层，那类输入走 PaddleOCR。用户没有指定文件或目录时，默认转换项目根目录下 `resources/data/` 里的文档。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-doc) · ★209 · 2 copies)
- **doudou-ocr**: 从图片和 PDF 里把文字提取成文本（OCR / 文字识别）。支持截图取字、扫描件与拍照文档识别、发票与票据取数、PDF 转文本、以及日英法德等 10 种语言的识别。当用户提供图片（png/jpg/bmp/gif/tiff/webp）或 PDF，并希望读出上面的文字、抄下截图里的内容、把扫描件或拍的照片变成可编辑文本、提取表单/发票/名片信息、或者让模型「看懂」一张图上写了什么时使用此技能。用户没有指定文件或目录时，默认识别项目根目录下 `resources/data/` 里的图片与 PDF。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-ocr) · ★209 · 2 copies)
- **doudou-product**: 将用户提供的需求资料或参考项目链接转化为面向产品、设计、研发和测试的完整产品设计交付物：产品需求文档（PRD）、设计交付说明、数据库表结构 SQL、研发实现说明和测试验收用例；当用户提供网站、Web 应用、后台系统、移动端 H5 或原型的参考链接时，使用 chrome-devtools-mcp 深入浏览和交互分析，并输出参考项目分析.md。用户提到“产品设计”、需求梳理、PRD、业务流程、功能流程、数据表设计、根据需求设计数据库、参考项目、竞品链接、分析这个网站/系统/页面或希望复刻某个产品功能时，主动使用此技能。开始前先识别资料来源（知识库、本地目录、参考链接或两两结合），阅读/分析资料后确认数据库类型及表名、字段名命名规范，再进行设计并输出完整交付物。用户未指定资料位置也未提供参考项目链接时，默认通过 doudou-wiki 技能从项目知识库（`kb/`）获取需求资料。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-product) · ★209 · 3 copies)
- **doudou-remotion-whiteboard**: Remotion 通用手绘白板与纸质动态动画引擎（Whiteboard & Hand-Drawn Motion Graphics）。支持白板马克笔/铅笔/粉笔/钢笔真实运笔跟随与抬笔落笔动力学、手绘几何图形与架构图箭头生长、素描排线阴影 (Cross-Hatch)、手写文本逐字显现、3D纸雕立体折叠、工程蓝图与黑板粉笔推导。适用于科技科普、产品解说、架构演进、课程教学、思维导图与商业白板视频。支持通过生图工具自动化生成实物免抠笔刷与特定纸质纹理底图。 ([AVENGERSTHE2102/videoeditor](https://github.com/AVENGERSTHE2102/videoeditor/tree/HEAD/library/undsky/doudou-remotion-whiteboard-skill/_root))
- **doudou-stt**: 音频/视频转成字幕或纯文本（Speech-to-Text / STT）。基于 WhisperX / faster-whisper，支持生成带时间轴的字幕（SRT/VTT）、提取逐字稿纯文本（TXT）、音素级强制对齐词级时间戳（JSON）、以及多说话人分离（Diarization）。当用户提供本地音视频文件或远程音频 URL，并希望获取语音内容、转录文本、提取字幕、生成 SRT/VTT、制作会议纪要逐字稿、区分说话人角色、或者获取词级时间戳时使用此技能。用户没有指定文件或目录时，默认转换项目根目录下 `resources/data/` 里的音视频文件。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-stt) · ★209 · 2 copies)
- **doudou-test**: 面向已获授权的 Web 应用质量与安全测试。每当用户说“开始测试 &lt;URL&gt;”、要求测试网页/前后端、黑盒或白盒测试、浏览器验收、接口验证、数据库影响分析，或希望生成测试报告时，都应使用本技能，即使用户没有明确说“技能”。技能会先让用户选择黑盒/白盒，再确认是否允许增删改操作，使用 Chrome DevTools MCP 进行浏览器测试，并在可访问源码和数据库结构时进行白盒分析；默认避免生产环境写操作。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-test) · ★209 · 3 copies)
- **doudou-wiki**: LLM 驱动的个人知识库（LLM Wiki）。把素材编译成一份持续复利的结构化 wiki： 收录素材、基于知识库回答问题、健康检查、重置删除。素材不限文本 —— 音视频、Office 文档、 PDF、扫描件、图片都能收，分别交给 doudou-stt / doudou-doc / doudou-ocr 技能预处理成文本再收录。 TRIGGER when: 用户提到「收录」「加到知识库」「归档这个」「add」，或让「把这段录音/这份合同/ 这张截图整理进知识库」，或提出知识库中可能有答案的问题（如「XX 是什么」「对比 XX 和 YY」 「整理一下 XX 的信息」），或要求「检查知识库」「体检」，或要求「清空」「删除」「重置」知识库， 或给出对知识库操作方式的反馈/偏好。 DO NOT TRIGGER when: 问题与知识库无关（纯代码任务、文件操作、通用对话），或用户只想单纯地 转个字幕/转个文档而不入库（那种直接用 doudou-stt / doudou-doc / doudou-ocr）， 或用户明确说不需要查知识库。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-wiki) · ★209 · 2 copies)
